# Stokastisk 0.1.0 — vLLM 0.25.1 Tesla T4 (SM75) compatibility matrix

**Method and limits.** Classification is by **static source inspection** (AST: imports, flash references, Triton decorators, capability gates, file names) plus the owner's **executed Kaggle evidence** (Triton kernel cubins in the acceptance ZIPs, mapped to source by `def <kernel>`; notebook outputs). A module is **SUPPORTED** only with direct Kaggle execution evidence. A module that merely imports is never SUPPORTED. Name-based SM80+ classification is a heuristic and is labelled as such. No test, benchmark or GPU run was performed for this report.

## Summary (1967 modules)
| Class | Count |
|---|---|
| SUPPORTED | 19 |
| SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | 537 |
| NEEDS_ADAPTATION | 10 |
| NOT_SUPPORTED_SM75 | 183 |
| EXCLUDED_FLASH_DEPENDENCY | 35 |
| NOT_IMPLEMENTED | 0 |
| UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | 1183 |
| **total** | **1967** |

`NOT_IMPLEMENTED` is 0 at module level (every classified file exists in the source); it is used for SDK-level features below (L4/SM89).

## Evidence tiers used
* `kaggle-kernel`: a Triton kernel defined in the file was compiled and launched on Kaggle 2×T4 (cubin present in an executed acceptance ZIP). Output of that feature was **not** necessarily asserted.
* `kaggle-path`: the file is on the path the notebooks exercised (vllm.LLM, Qwen2, TRITON_ATTN, TP/PP communicator, sampler).
* `static-import-closure`: reachable by top-level imports from the executed entry points (`vllm.entrypoints.llm`, `v1.engine.core`, `v1.engine.llm_engine`, `v1.worker.gpu_worker`, `multiproc_executor`, `models.qwen2`, `triton_attn`, `parallel_state`): 638 modules. Reachable ≠ executed.
* `wheel-vs-stage diff`, `static`: source facts only.

## Feature matrix (`stokastisk.compat.FEATURES`)
| Feature | Status | Evidence | Notebooks | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| offline_generation | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 05,06,08,09,11,13,14 | vllm.LLM generate() with Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B-Instruct, fp16, enforce_eager, TRITON_ATTN |
| batch_generation | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 09,11,14 | batches of 1..64 requests, 64 output tokens |
| triton_attention | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 05,13 | TRITON_ATTN selected and confirmed in the live VllmConfig; kernel_unified_attention compiled; no Flash library mapped in any process |
| tensor_parallel_2 | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 05,06,13 | both GPUs hold a shard; slower than TP=1 for a model that fits one T4 (about 0.55x decode) |
| pipeline_parallel_2 | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | kaggle | 08,13 | experimental in this SDK; both GPUs hold stages; about 0.6x of TP=1 decode for a model that fits one T4 |
| replica_parallel | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | kaggle | 09,14 | SDK feature (ReplicaPool): one TP=1 engine per GPU, both receive work; no throughput gain over one engine up to batch 64 |
| gpu_telemetry | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 09,11,14 | nvidia-smi sampling during benchmarks |
| topology_discovery | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 01,07 | PHB; CUDA peer access measured per session (true in one Kaggle VM, false in another) |
| nccl_collectives | SUPPORTED | kaggle | 04 | all_reduce/all_gather/reduce_scatter/broadcast on both ranks |
| sampling_temperature_top_k_top_p | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | kaggle-kernel |  | kernels compiled during runs; only greedy (temperature 0) output was asserted |
| logprobs | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | kaggle-kernel |  | logprob / prompt-logprob kernels compiled; output not asserted |
| penalties_min_p_bad_words_logit_bias | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | kaggle-kernel |  | kernels compiled; output not asserted |
| chat_generation | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | vllm.LLM.chat exists; exposed as stokastisk.LLM.chat; chat templates and tokenizer not run on Kaggle |
| openai_server | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | implemented-not-executed |  | vllm serve exists in the wheel; stokastisk.serving launches it; needs fastapi/uvicorn/openai etc. that the protected Kaggle image may not provide |
| streaming | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | implemented-not-executed |  | available through the OpenAI server (stream=true); offline LLM does not stream |
| prometheus_metrics | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | served at /metrics by the OpenAI server |
| structured_outputs | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | kaggle-kernel |  | grammar-bitmask kernel compiled; xgrammar/llguidance runs not exercised |
| prefix_caching | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | no run asserts it |
| chunked_prefill | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | no run asserts it |
| cuda_graphs | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | every Kaggle run used enforce_eager=True (the SDK default); graph capture on T4 is unverified |
| custom_all_reduce | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | disabled by the SDK default; needs CUDA peer access, which differs between Kaggle VMs |
| lora | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | present in the source; no run |
| multimodal | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | present in the source; no run |
| embedding_pooling_reranking | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | present in the source; no run |
| speculative_decoding | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | present in the source; no run |
| mla_models_deepseek | UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE | static |  | FlashMLA/FlashInfer-MLA backends are excluded; other MLA backends were not run |
| worker_failure_recovery | SUPPORTED_WITH_CONDITIONS | cpu-tests |  | ReplicaPool restarts/retries covered by CPU tests with fake workers only |
| bfloat16 | NOT_SUPPORTED_SM75 | static |  | vllm/platforms/cuda.py rejects bfloat16 below compute capability 8.0; use dtype='half' |
| flash_attention | EXCLUDED_FLASH_DEPENDENCY | static |  | FlashAttention is not shipped and not allowed |
| flashinfer | EXCLUDED_FLASH_DEPENDENCY | static |  | FlashInfer attention, sampler and all-reduce fusion are not shipped |
| flashmla | EXCLUDED_FLASH_DEPENDENCY | static |  | FlashMLA is not shipped |
| allreduce_rms_fusion | EXCLUDED_FLASH_DEPENDENCY | static |  | needs FlashInfer comm; pass_manager raises a clear error if requested |
| fp8_mxfp4_nvfp4_native | NOT_SUPPORTED_SM75 | static |  | native FP8/FP4 kernels need SM89/SM90/SM100 (see check_quantization for the per-method minimum declared in the source) |
| expert_parallel_all2all | NOT_SUPPORTED_SM75 | static |  | DeepEP / NIXL / FlashInfer all2all backends are not shipped or need newer GPUs |
| qwen_omni_models | NEEDS_ADAPTATION | static |  | qwen2_5_omni_thinker was removed from the T4 wheel by the module-level flash-import rule (its flash_attn import is guarded); restoring it needs a new vLLM wheel |
| l4_sm89 | NOT_IMPLEMENTED | static |  | planned only; no SM89 binaries, no support claim |

## Quantization methods (minimum capability declared by `get_min_capability()` in the shipped source)
| Method | Minimum | Source file | T4 (7.5) | Run on Kaggle |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| awq | 7.5 | `auto_awq.py` | meets minimum | no |
| auto_awq | 7.5 | `auto_awq.py` | meets minimum | no |
| gptq | 6.0 | `auto_gptq.py` | meets minimum | no |
| auto_gptq | 6.0 | `auto_gptq.py` | meets minimum | no |
| bitsandbytes | 7.0 | `bitsandbytes.py` | meets minimum | no |
| compressed-tensors | 7.0 | `compressed_tensors/compressed_tensors.py` | meets minimum | no |
| moe_wna16 | 7.0 | `moe_wna16.py` | meets minimum | no |
| torchao | 7.5 | `torchao.py` | meets minimum | no |
| humming | 7.5 | `humming.py` | meets minimum | no |
| fp8 | 7.5 | `fp8.py` | meets minimum | no |
| experts_int8 | 8.0 | `experts_int8.py` | BELOW minimum — refused by check_quantization | no |
| fbgemm_fp8 | 8.0 | `fbgemm_fp8.py` | BELOW minimum — refused by check_quantization | no |
| mxfp4 | 8.0 | `mxfp4.py` | BELOW minimum — refused by check_quantization | no |
| fp_quant | 10.0 | `fp_quant.py` | BELOW minimum — refused by check_quantization | no |

Marlin-based methods (`gptq_marlin`, `awq_marlin`) declare no minimum in the inspected files; they are reported UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE by the SDK.

## Model architectures (`stokastisk.compat.check_model`, static)
Of 356 architectures registered in vLLM 0.25.1: **346** pass the static module/import check (all UNKNOWN_REQUIRES_EVIDENCE except Qwen2ForCausalLM = SUPPORTED by Kaggle runs) and **10** are blocked because the module, or an unconditional import below it, was removed from the T4 wheel: `Qwen2_5OmniModel`, `Qwen2_5OmniForConditionalGeneration`, `Qwen3OmniMoeForConditionalGeneration`, `Qwen3ASRForConditionalGeneration`, `Qwen3ASRForcedAlignerForTokenClassification`, `Qwen3ASRRealtimeGeneration`, `PanguEmbeddedForCausalLM`, `PanguProMoEV2ForCausalLM`, `PanguUltraMoEForCausalLM`, `OpenPanguMTPModel`. (Evidence: static; this check passing does not mean the model loads or runs on a T4.)
